**Done when:** core tests: message only at an airport from the table, TOBT = plan + delay, update after `DELAY`, empty after TOBT, removed at OUT, "suspended" and the return; limits of all displays (`checkVdgsLines`); GSX file with `id: flight_information` (test `apps/desktop/test/vdgs.test.ts`); replay; `pnpm check` is green.

**Human check:** MSFS 2024 + GSX Pro with a SimBrief login, an airport from the table with a TS-24/TS-42 stand (for example, EDDL, if GSX installs such displays there), aircraft at the departure stand: 1) the display shows the flight and the ACARS Link TOBT instead of the SimBrief TOBT; 2) after `DELAY 20` TOBT shifts by 20 min on the next page cycle; 3) after pushback there is no message; 4) whether the display appears if the aircraft is loaded straight onto the stand and the GSX menu has not been opened yet.

#### T103 — VDGS display at departure outside A-CDM: ETD (P2)
User request 2026-11-05: so that the departure display works at airports not in the A-CDM table (T102) too, even without confirmation by reality.

**Approved by the user (2026-11-05), assumptions — not a reproduction of a real display:** 1) a separate scenario `sim/vdgs_departure_etd`, **disabled by default**, with the note "not a real display" in the title and description; 2) override of the GSX `flight_information` at airports not in `ext/acdm-airports.ts`, the T102 layout (flight, destination, runway, SID); 3) instead of TOBT — **ETD** = `ofp.est_out` (all delays, **including the slot**) and the `TTD` countdown — labels of the standard GSX message; from ETD − 30 min, after ETD the line is empty; 4) no FLIGHT SUSPENDED (an A-CDM procedure). Rejected: a display without time (barely differs from the standard GSX).

**Done:** the scenario, variable `flight.minutes_to_etd`, tests `scenarios/test/sim.test.ts` (not in A-CDM, ETD and countdown, empty after ETD, no SUSPENDED, removed at OUT, shift after `DELAY`).

**Human check:** as T102, at an airport not in the table (for example LTFM or KJFK), with the scenario enabled in settings: the ACARS Link ETD instead of the SimBrief time; after `DELAY 20` the ETD shifts.

### Emergency services on the ground (task card T88)

#### T88 — MAYDAY / PAN on the ground: services at the airport where the aircraft stands **R**
User request 2026-11-04 (check of the installed 1.0.0 at KHOU). Currently a MAYDAY / PAN declared on the ground behaves as in flight, and there are no vehicles in the simulator:
- `set flight.divert` is accepted only between OFF and ON (`engine.ts`, `assign`): on the ground DIVERT is silently not recorded, the airline replies `ROGER DIVERT NEAREST SUITABLE` without an airport;
- the `rescue` step takes `flight.divert`, otherwise `ofp.dest`: services go to the destination airport (in recording `20261101-111907-DAL8955` — KBUR 08 with the aircraft at KHOU);
- the bridge places vehicles at ≤ 15 NM from the threshold, and they start surrounding the aircraft only if it was airborne (`RescueController`, `Staged` → `_airborne`): on the ground the vehicles would stand at the runway to the end;
- scenarios on the ground ask DIVERT / CONTINUE.

**"On the ground":** before OFF or after ON (OOOI). **The airport where the aircraft stands:** the nearest within 5 NM among the flight's airports — departure, destination, alternates, diversion (the landing airport rule of the T69 summary, `summary/report.ts`).

**Core and scenarios**
1. A variable for scenario conditions — the airport where the aircraft stands (`flight.here`, null — in the air or no airport within 5 NM); the name — at implementation.
2. `emergency_mayday`, `emergency_pan`: on the ground — their own branch without the DIVERT / CONTINUE question (no reply awaited). MAYDAY text — `MAYDAY RCVD VIA ATC / EMERGENCY DESK ACTIVATED / RESCUE AND FIRE SERVICES ALERTED AT {airport} / ADVISE INTENTIONS`; PAN — the same with `RESCUE AND MEDICAL STANDBY AT {airport}`. Hints and translations in `locales/<lang>.yaml` — as for the other steps (T85).
3. The `rescue` step on the ground — to the airport where the aircraft stands; in the air — as now (`flight.divert`, otherwise `ofp.dest`). After landing with no diversion assigned (for example, a return to the departure airport) — also the airport where the aircraft stands.
4. Scenario version and core option — as is customary for behaviour changes (old recordings play back the old way, `recordedCoreOptions`).

**Application**
5. `rescue_start` gets a ground-call flag (`ground`, bridge protocol v2, the `CommandCatalog` whitelist and `checkSimCommand` — both ends); the runway — as now (`landingRunway`: the planned one, otherwise into the wind by METAR).

**Bridge (`RescueController`, under tests)**
6. Ground call: vehicles are created at the runway, as now (≤ 15 NM from the threshold), and immediately drive to the aircraft the same way as to the circle after a stop (straight or around via an arc, `CircleKt`, stopping by `NoProgressAfter` / `CircleDriveMax`). `full` — surround the aircraft (`Encircle`, removed 10 min after arrival); `standby` — park nearby, to the side, without surrounding. The ambulance — as now: at the aircraft on the stand with the parking brake.
7. The aircraft moved during a ground call — vehicles follow it, like `Trailing` after touchdown (`full`); `standby` — wait.

**Assumptions (approved by the user 2026-11-04):**
- A1. On the ground vehicles appear at the runway and drive to the aircraft across the airfield (not next to the aircraft and not straight into a circle). The route — straight or an arc, without taxiways: up to the gate this can be 1–3 km through buildings and aircraft; the pilot will see it.
- A2. MAYDAY on the ground is an OPS message without DIVERT / CONTINUE (the aircraft is already at an airport).
- A3. PAN on the ground is the `standby` level: vehicles drive up and stand nearby, not surrounding; the ambulance — at the aircraft on the stand.
- A4. After landing without a diversion, services are called to where the aircraft stands (5 NM, as T69), not to the destination airport.

**Open questions (before implementation):**
1. The aircraft is on the ground, but there is no flight airport within 5 NM (flight KHOU–KBUR, the aircraft stands at LEBL): the proposal — no services command, a feed line "no flight airport nearby"; the OPS message — without `AT {airport}`.
2. In recording `20261101-111907-DAL8955` the `emergency_mayday` chain ran twice on a single `ROGER MAYDAY` (messages at 11:41 and 11:44 UTC): find out whether the second run was manual ("Run now") or a core repeat.

**Dependency:** T47 (MAYDAY/PAN chain), T54 (bridge), T69 (airport within 5 NM).

**Done when:** core tests (MAYDAY / PAN before OFF, after ON without a diversion, in the air — unchanged), `RescueController` tests (ground `full` and `standby`, the aircraft moved), protocol tests (`ground` in `rescue_start`); replay of an old recording gives the same result as before; `pnpm check` and `pnpm bridge:test` are green.

**Human check:** 1) MSFS, aircraft on a stand at KHOU, OFP from KHOU, XATC on: LOGON `XZHU`, CPDLC MAYDAY → `ROGER MAYDAY`, then OPS `… ALERTED AT KHOU` without a DIVERT question; fire trucks appear at the runway and drive to the aircraft, surround it; after 10 min they disappear. 2) The same with PAN: vehicles park nearby, not surrounding; the ambulance at the aircraft with the parking brake set. 3) Takeoff, return and landing at KHOU without DIVERT, then MAYDAY on the ground — services at KHOU. 4) MAYDAY in flight — as before (DIVERT / CONTINUE question, services at the diversion airport).

#### T104 — Smoke package in the installer
User request 2026-11-05: so that the T34 smoke is available to everyone, not only to those who built the package themselves; the "install via the installer" option was chosen. The T54/T88 vehicles are standard MSFS objects, they do not need the package.

**Obstacle (verified 2026-11-05 against `MSFS SDK EULA.pdf` from SDK 2024 1.8.1, edition 11/2019, item 1 b):** the SDK samples are for internal use only, external distribution is prohibited. The texture and effect graph of the current smoke are taken from SimpleFX — they must not be put into the installer (this lifts the `[VERIFY]` of T34).

**Plan:** 1) our own texture; 2) the effect built anew in the Visual Effects Editor from an empty one, on our texture, our own GUIDs (a human, DevMode); 3) the built package `avail-acars-smoke` — into the repository (it cannot be built without the SDK, there is no SDK in CI), into the installer via `extraResources`; 4) copying to Community by the application with the user's consent, updating by the `manifest.json` version, removal by a button. Item 4 was approved by the user 2026-11-05; item 3 — after the effect is built.

**User decision (2026-11-05):** the texture is generated by a script. **Done:** `tools/smoke-texture/generate.mjs` — a single 512×512 RGBA puff (format like the SimpleFX smoke texture: one frame, no atlas, straight alpha), white with soft self-shading from the top left, alpha — fBm noise with coordinate warping and a radial falloff, the edge (9 px) fully transparent, maximum alpha 242; seed 104, the same result for the same seed. The file — `bridge/msfs/package/PackageSources/MaterialLibs/acars-smoke-materiallib/Textures/acars_smoke.png`; the test `tools/smoke-texture/test` checks it against the generator output.

**Done (item 4, 2026-11-05):** `apps/desktop/src/main/smoke-package.ts` — Community lookup by the rules of the MSFS 2024 SDK sample `Samples/VisualStudio/PackageInstaller/DetectMSFS/CustomAction.cpp` (SDK 1.8.1, verified 2026-11-05): `UserCfg.opt` Store — `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Packages\Microsoft.Limitless_8wekyb3d8bbwe\LocalCache`, Steam — `%APPDATA%\Microsoft Flight Simulator 2024`; the line `InstalledPackagesPath "<folder>"`, the folder must exist, Community — `<folder>\Community`; if both versions are found, the package is installed into each (as in the sample). Installation only by a button ("Advanced settings" → "Smoke package for MSFS 2024": state per version, "Install" / "Update" / "Remove"): a copy alongside (`<name>.acars-tmp`), then a replacement; the same or a newer version is not touched; a folder with the same name and a different `title` in `manifest.json` or without a manifest is foreign, not replaced and not removed. The package in the installer — `extraResources` from `bridge/msfs/package/dist/avail-acars-smoke` → `resources/avail-acars-smoke` (no folder — electron-builder skips it, the build does not fail); from sources — the same repository folder. IPC `getSmokePackage` / `installSmokePackage` / `removeSmokePackage`, strings `set.smokePkg*` (DE/FR/ES/JA — machine translation). Tests: `apps/desktop/test/smoke-package.test.ts`. Checked by reading on the user's machine: Steam found, `D:\MSFS2024\Community`.

**Human check:** in the Visual Effects Editor build an effect on `acars_smoke.png` (a new material, BlendMode Transparent), the colour — the effect curve; the smoke column at the runway (`dep_fire` "Run now", LEBL) looks like smoke, without square particle edges.
**Guide for items 2–3 (2026-11-05):** `docs/guide-smoke-package.md` — a new project and an effect from an empty one, the material `AcarsSmoke` with our own texture, the object and the package `avail-acars-smoke` (Title `ACARS Smoke`), a check via the "Install" button. Found during preparation: the XML of the working T34 effect has no reference to a material — it was probably drawn with the default Asobo material, not the SimpleFX texture. `[VERIFY]` the material and effect in one package (in the SDK sample — different packages). **Package name (user decision 2026-11-05):** `avail-acars-smoke` — the Project Editor itself added the default company `avail` to the name; the application (`SMOKE_PACKAGE`) and the installer were renamed to match, the package is not renamed.
**Effect graph (at the user's request 2026-11-05):** written by a script into `PackageSources\VisualEffectLibscars-smoke\VisualEffectLibcars-smoke.xml` of the user's project `D:\msfs-projectscars-smoke`: the wizard's nodes (VisualEffect, Emitter, Init, Update, Output, GravityVector, Vector4) with their ids, the other 27 — new GUIDs; numbers, curves and links — T34 (verified by unfolding the Emitter/Init/Update/Output inputs: 9 of 9 match). The material on Output is set by a human. A copy of the wizard's files — `D:\msfs-projectscars-smoke\_backup-effect-wizard`.
**Colour with a material (found 2026-11-05):** with a `CODE_DIFFUSE` material on Output the Color (RGBA) input does not change the colour — the smoke is white; it is dark if the material's Diffuse Color is dark (checked by a human, `0.08`). Without a texture in Albedo the particles are not visible.
**Plume realism — approved by the user 2026-11-05 (tuned by eye, not measurements):** 1) a wider base: birth size 20 m (was 9), spread from the start of life — curve B (0; 1) (0.3; 1.5) (0.6; 2) (1; 2.5) (was 0.3 → 5); 2) no "cap": size growth ×50 (was ×90), transparency (0; 0) (0.02; 0.75) (0.3; 0.65) (0.7; 0.4) (1; 0); 3) wind drift: WindDirection `Raw` (ft/s), × 0.305 × curve D as before; 4) colour per particle via the Output input `Material Diffuse`, grey (0; 0.03) (0.3; 0.08) (0.6; 0.16) (1; 0.25) × random 0.7–1.2, the material's Diffuse Color — white; 5) maximum transparency 0.75 (was 0.9). The `Raw` and `Material Diffuse` fields are set by a human in the editor, the numbers — by a script.
**Tuning result (2026-11-05):** edits 1, 2, 5 were applied by a script to the effect file; 3) wind drift works (checked by a human): `WindDirOptions` `Raw` — the default value, not written to the XML; 4) **replaced by the user's decision 2026-11-05:** the Color (RGBA) and Material Diffuse inputs of Output with a `CODE_DIFFUSE` material do not change the colour (the smoke is white with both connected) — the colour is constant, the material's Diffuse Color `0.1, 0.1, 0.1`, the Material Diffuse input removed; there is no lightening with height. A possible future experiment — a different VFX material type.
**Item 3 done (2026-11-05):** the package `avail-acars-smoke` 1.0.0 was built by a human in the MSFS 2024 SDK (project `D:\msfs-projectscars-smoke`: the effect, the material `AcarsSmoke` with our own texture and colour 0.1, the object `Landmarks\ACARS_Smoke`, `FX_GUID` = the effect's InstanceId) and lies in `bridge/msfs/package/dist/avail-acars-smoke` (an exception in `.gitignore`); without `Library.xml` in the sources of the material group the package does not build (created by hand — an empty wrapper). The `ContentInfo` thumbnail is an SDK wizard placeholder, left by the user's decision until the next package version. **Checked by a human:** installation via the "Install" button works, the smoke is visible, dark, drifts with the wind.
**Human check (item 4, after item 3):** `pnpm desktop:dist`, install, MSFS closed; Advanced settings → "Smoke package for MSFS 2024" — "MSFS 2024 (Steam): not installed"; "Install" → "Done — restart MSFS", the package is in `D:\MSFS2024\Communityvail-acars-smoke`; start MSFS, `dep_fire` at LEBL — smoke; "Remove" — the folder is gone.

### User requests 2026-11-06 (cards T105–T107)

Comments on release 1.1.0: "weight is not in kilograms" (T105), "can I choose whether to show CTOT" (T106), "free text to the pilot and OPS" (T107, not started: OPS replies without an LLM — intent recognition; with an LLM — after T16, invariant 8).

#### T105 — Weight units in messages (KG / LB)
User request (comment on release 1.1.0): weights not in kilograms. Previously: an OFP in pounds was converted to kg, and all messages went out in tonnes / KG.
**Sources (2026-11-06):** a real ACARS loadsheet printout in pounds was not found in open sources — there are only abbreviations (ZFW/TOF/TOW/TIF/LAW) and Boeing tables in both units. 1 lb = 0.45359237 kg — exact, by definition (1959).
**Assumptions (approved by the user 2026-11-06):** 1) the LB format is the same as in tonnes: large numbers in thousands of pounds with one decimal (`ZFW 397.3`), the line `ALL WEIGHTS IN 1000 LBS` / `FUEL IN 1000 LBS`, small ones (LMC, DIFF, FOB, EXTRA) — whole LB; lines with a unit (`BLOCK FUEL 12.3 T`) in LB — whole pounds to the hundred (`27100 LB`; derived from item 1, precision like `w1`); 2) the setting "Weight units" — `As in the OFP` (default) / `KG` / `LB`.
**Done:** core option `massUnit` (`context.ts`: `units.mass`, `units.mass_big`), formatters `w1` / `w` / `wt` (`template.ts`, docs/05), templates base, irregular, cargo, ferry, bizav; setting `massUnit` (`settings.ts`, `SettingsView.tsx`, group "Messages", DE/FR/ES/JA — `pnpm i18n translate --only msg`); `massUnit` in the recording header (without the field — `kg`, old recordings play as before). Tests: `t105.test.ts`, the "lbs OFP" runs in base and irregular are now in LB (line width of all profiles), formatters — `scenario-expr-template.test.ts`.
**Not included:** weights in the application UI (the T69 flight summary and others); `dg_notoc` (kg per IATA DGR).
**Human check:** 1) SimBrief: OFP in LBS; in the application "Weight units" — "As in the OFP"; a flight up to the loadsheets — in the FMS/MFD `ALL WEIGHTS IN 1000 LBS`, ZFW ≈ ZFW from the OFP / 1000. 2) The same OFP with "Kilograms (KG)" — `ALL WEIGHTS IN TONNES`. 3) Loadsheet lines do not wrap on the aircraft display (A320/737/777/787).

#### T106 — How to turn off one kind of message (CTOT)
Scenario switches (`Settings.scenarios`) and search on the "Scenarios" tab (by id, title and description) already existed — the user did not find them. **User decision 2026-11-06:** search and help, no separate CTOT group switch. **Done:** in the help (question `settings.scenarios`) — a paragraph: search by a word from the message ("CTOT" finds `slot_ctot`, `slot_missed`, `atc_flow_delay`), the message subject in the feed is the scenario title; DE/FR/ES/JA — `pnpm i18n translate --only help`.
**Human check:** the "Scenarios" tab, `CTOT` in search — three scenarios; Help → "Can I choose which messages arrive?" — the new paragraph.

#### T107 — Charter type: tour, sports, VIP **R** (P2)
On a charter (T91) the tour operator, sports team and VIP stories were played at random (group `charter_story`), while the "late group" (`charter_group_lmc`) and the transfer (`charter_transfer`) arrived on any charter: on a tour flight there could be `SPORTS TEAM ON BOARD`, on a VIP — `TOUR GROUP PAX NO SHOW`.

**Decided (2026-11-07, user):** 1) the types — `tour` (tour), `sports` (sports team), `vip` — per the T91 stories, no new texts; 2) the user picks the type in a second list next to "Kind of flight" (only with "Charter"), not chosen — tour; it is fixed at OUT, like the kind of flight; 3) the "late group" and the transfer — only on tour; 4) when a type is chosen manually — a story of that type always arrives (`charter_probability: 1`), for tour by default — 30 %, as it was; the probabilities of the group and the transfer (50 %, 60 %) do not change.

**Done (2026-11-07):** `CHARTER_TYPES`, `DEFAULT_CHARTER_TYPE`, `KindChoice` — `packages/core/src/scenario/flight-kind.ts`; `classifyFlight(ofp, override, charter)` → `FlightKindInfo.charter` / `charterOverride`. Scenario fields `charter` (only with `operations: [charter_pax]`) and `charter_probability` (`types.ts`, `compile.ts`, schema `packages/scenarios/src/schema.ts`, docs/05). Engine: the type filter in `wrongKind` (a manual run — also `operation`), the probability — `probability(sc)`; core option and input `flightKind: { override, charter }`, event `FLIGHT_KIND` with `charter`. Without `charter` in the option (recordings before T107) the type is not checked and probabilities are as before — old recordings play back as they were. Recording: `flightKind.charter` in the header and in the `tick` line (`tools/replay/src/format.ts`, `run.ts`; a synthetic flight — `charter: null`). Application: `setFlightKind(kind, charter?)` (`controller.ts`, `session.ts`, IPC), the "Type of charter" list in `KindBlock` (`FlightView.tsx`), "not for this type of charter" in "Run now" (`ScenariosView.tsx`); strings `charter.*` — EN/RU, DE/FR/ES/JA — translated by hand (without `pnpm i18n`). Tests — `packages/scenarios/test/t107.test.ts`, `load.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/test/controller.test.ts`.

**Not included:** charter type in the T69 flight summary and the logbook; own texts of the group and transfer for sports and VIP.

**Human check:** 1) Load an OFP, "Kind of flight" → "Charter": a "Type of charter" list appeared = "Default: Tour (package holiday)". A flight up to TOD — no `SPORTS TEAM` and `VIP CHARTER` messages. 2) A new flight, "Charter" + "Sports team": about 45 min before STD `SPORTS TEAM ON BOARD` arrives; no `TOUR GROUP`, `TOUR OPERATOR REP`, `TRANSFER COACHES`. 3) After OUT both lists are unavailable. 4) "Run now" on a sports charter: "Tour group change" is marked "not for this type of charter".

## Timing guidelines
- Stage 1: 3–4 weeks.
- Stage 2: 3–4 weeks (the core feature set with a clean architecture: ~6–8 weeks in total).
- Stage 3: 6–10 weeks.
- Stage 4: as needed.
- Stage 5: priority 1 — in the order T21–T29 (T30 cancelled), priority 2 — after.
- Stage 6: T39 → T40 → T41 → T43, T44 → T46 → T47 → T48 → T45; T42 in parallel; T50 — after T48 (waiting via XATC) and T40 (aircraft family); T51 — after T50; T52 — independent (the profile at once, the rest — by human check). T53 — independent. T54 — after T50 (diversion airport) and T40 (aircraft family). T61 — independent, after the assumptions are approved. T62 — independent (decisions made 2026-10-28). Audit 2026-10-28: T63 — first (until it, the code has no backup); T65, T66, T68 — independent, no user decisions; T67 — before T64 (logs are needed to analyse recovery); T64 — after T67; T69 — independent, after the decisions are approved. Audit 2026-10-31: T72 — after a real OFP across the Atlantic and decisions 1–2; T73 — independent, item 1 (the official SDK instead of Skybound copies) — first; T76 item 1 (timeout in CI) — at once, in a single edit; T77 — after decisions 1–3; T75 — after decisions 1–2; T74 — after T72 (the composition of the source list depends on the build variant). Audit 2026-10-31 (second): T78 — first (until it, T72–T77 have no backup); T79 — before 2026-11-10 (end of .NET 8 support); T80, T81 — independent, after the decisions; T82 — after T79; T83 — in parallel (the user writes the letters), before the installer is handed to other people; T84 — last, after T78–T82. Localization: T85 → T86 → T87, after the user's decisions. Flight kinds: T89 first (without it the packages have nothing to filter by), then T90, T91, T92 in any order after the user's decisions; T93 — after T88 and on request. The timeline is determined by agreeing the assumptions (**R**), not by code.
- Development ideas 2026-11-05 (T94–T101): independent of each other, all — after the assumptions and game numbers are agreed. P1 first: T95 (OFP only, no new sources), T94, T98 (GSX variables must be verified). Then P2: T101 (after T42), T100 (after T89), T99 (after T39, T48), T97, T96. T102 (VDGS display at departure) — independent, after the user's decisions and verification of the A-CDM airport list.
